Perturbation scale decreases as the run progresses\n   289\t                sigma_local = (ub - lb) * max(1e-7, 0.01 * (1.0 - 0.5 * progress))\n   290\t                for _ in range(local_search_n):\n   291\t                    if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   292\t                        break\n   293\t                    cand = self._reflect(gbest + sigma_local * rng.randn(self.dim), lb, ub)\n   294\t                    cand_f = evaluate_problem(problem, cand)\n   295\t                    evals += 1\n   296\t                    if cand_f < gbest_f:\n   297\t                        gbest_f = cand_f\n   298\t                        gbest = cand.copy()\n   299\t                        # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n   329\t            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n   330\t            \n   331\t            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n   332\t            if self.controller is not None and train:\n   333\t                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n   334\t                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n   335\t                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n   336\t                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n   337\t                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n   338\t            \n   339\t            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n   340\t\n\"\n    }\n    </json>"

\"\"\"\u52a0\u8f7d dataset.csv \u6587\u4ef6\uff0c\u8fd4\u56de\u4e00\u4e2a\u5b57\u5178 {(function, instance, dimension): opt_val}\"\"\"\n    dataset_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__),     key = (int(row['function']), int(row['instance']), int(row['dimension']))\n                opt_vals[key] = float(row['opt_val'])\n    return opt_vals\n\n\ndef compute_ri_score(initial_gbest_f, gbest_f, opt_val):\n    \"\"\"\u8ba1\u7b97 score = (initial_best - best) / (initial_best - opt)\u3002\n    \n    \u5176\u4e2d\uff1a\n    - initial_best = initial_gbest_f\uff08PSO\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u540e\u79cd\u7fa4\u7684\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    - best = gbest_f\uff08PSO\u8fd0\u884c\u7ed3\u675f\u540e\u7684\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    - opt = 1.0 if gbest_f <= opt_val + 1e-12 else 0.0\n    \n    score = (initial_gbest_f - gbest_f) / (initial_gbest_f - opt_val)\n    return max(0.0, min(1.0, score))\n\n\ndef run_de_and_score(inputs, log_fn=print, mode='test', controller=None, seed=None):\n    \"\"\"\u8fd0\u884c DQN \u63a7\u5236\u7684 DE \u5e76\u8ba1\u7b97 RI \u8bc4\u5206\u3002\n    \n    Args:\n        inputs: dict from domains.bbob_unconstrained.utils.format_input_dict\n        Expected keys: 'problem_id','dimension','instance'\n        log_fn: logging function\n        mode: 'train' for training DQN (updates model), 'test' for evaluation only\n        controller: optional external controller (shared across problems)\n        seed: random seed for DE initialization\n    \n    Returns:\n        prediction importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)\n    spec.loader.exec_module(ec_mod)\n    \n    # Create or use provided controller\n    if controller is None:\n        # load meta learning implementations\n spec2.loader.exec_module(meta_mod)\n        model_path = os.path.join(this_dir, 'dqn_model.pt')\n        controller = meta_mod.DiscreteDQNController(model_path=model_path, training=(mode == 'train'), seed=seed)\n    \n    # prepare suite filter (dimension + instance)\n    dim = int(inputs.get('dimension', 10))\n    inst = int(inputs.get('instance', 1))\n    max_evals = max(1000, dim * 500)\n\n    # \u4ece problem_id \u5b57\u7b26\u4e32\u4e2d\u63d0\u53d6 function \u7f16\u53f7\uff08\u4f8b\u5982 f1_i1 -> 1\uff09\n    problem_id_str = inputs.get('problem_id', 'f1_i1')\n    problem_id = int(str(problem_id_str).replace('f', '').split('_')[0])\n\n    suite = cocoex.Suite('bbob', '', f'dimensions: {dim} instance_indices:{inst} function_indices: {problem_id}')\n    # pick the matching problem from the suite\n    problem = next(iter(suite))\n\n    # run DE algorithm using DEOptimizer to get stats\n    try:\n        de_opt = ec_mod.DEOptimizer(problem.dimension, getattr(problem, 'lower_bounds', None), getattr(problem, 'upper_bounds', None), max_evals, params={'F': 0.5, 'CR': 0.5}, controller=controller, recorder=None, swarm_size=40)\n        # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4f1a\u66f4\u65b0 DQN \u6a21\u578b\uff0c\u6d4b\u8bd5\u6a21\u5f0f\u53ea\u4f7f\u7528\u6a21\u578b\n        gbest, stats = de_opt.optimize(problem, train=(mode == 'train'), seed=seed)\n        gbest_f = stats.get('gbest_f', None)\n        initial_gbest_f = stats.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f)\n        \n        # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4e0b\u4fdd\u5b58\u6a21\u578b\uff08\u53ea\u5728 controller \u662f\u5185\u90e8\u521b\u5efa\u65f6\u4fdd\u5b58\uff09\n        if mode == 'train' and controller is not None and hasattr(controller, 'net'):\n            import torch\n            model_path = os.path.join(this_dir, 'dqn_model.pt')\n            torch.save(controller.net.state_dict(), model_path)\n            log_fn(f\"[TRAIN] Model saved to {model_path}\")\n    except Exception as e:\n        return f'error: DE run failed: {e}'\n\n    # \u8ba1\u7b97 RI \u5206\u6570\n    # \u52a0\u8f7d\u7406\u8bba\u6700\u4f18\u503c\n    opt_vals = load_opt_vals()\n    opt_val_key = (problem_id, inst, dim)\n    opt_val = opt_vals.get(opt_val_key, None)\n    \n    # \u4f7f\u7528 RI \u8bc4\u5206\u65b9\u5f0f\n    score = compute_ri_score(initial_gbest_f, gbest_f, opt_val)\n    \n    mode_str = \"TRAIN\" if mode == 'train' else \"TEST\"\n    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} Score: {score}\")\n    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} gbest_f: {gbest_f}\")\n    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} initial_gbest_f: {initial_gbest_f}\")\n    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} opt_val: {opt_val}\")\n    \n    # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4e0b\u6536\u96c6 loss \u548c return\n    if mode == 'train':\n        training_loss = controller.get_total_loss() if hasattr(controller, 'get_total_loss') else None\n        sweep_return = controller.get_total_reward() if hasattr(controller, 'get_total_reward') else None\n        if training_loss is not None:\n            log_fn(f\"[TRAIN] Total DQN loss: {training_loss:.6f}\")\n        if sweep_return is not None:\n            log_fn(f\"[TRAIN] Total DQN return: {sweep_return:.4f}\")\n        return {\n            'score': score,\n            'gbest_f': gbest_f,\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n            'opt_val': opt_val,\n            'training_loss': training_loss,\n            'sweep_return': sweep_return,\n            'controller': controller\n        }\n    \n    # \u8fd4\u56de\u683c\u5f0f\uff1a\"score|gbest_f|initial_gbest_f|opt_val\" \u4ee5\u4fbf report.py \u89e3\u6790\n    prediction = f\"{score}|{gbest_f}|{initial_gbest_f}|{opt_val}\"\n\n    # \u4e5f\u8fd4\u56de dict \u683c\u5f0f\u4f9b\u6d4b\u8bd5\u811a\u672c\u4f7f\u7528\n    return {\n \"\"\"\u52a0\u8f7d dataset.csv \u6587\u4ef6\uff0c\u8fd4\u56de\u4e00\u4e2a\u5b57\u5178 {(function, instance, dimension): opt_val}\"\"\"\n    dataset_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__),     key = (int(row['function']), int(row['instance']), int(row['dimension']))\n                opt_vals[key] = float(row['opt_val'])\n    return opt_vals\n\n\ndef compute_ri_score(initial_gbest_f, gbest_f, opt_val):\n    \"\"\"\u8ba1\u7b97 score = (initial_best - best) / (initial_best - opt)\u3002\n    \n    \u5176\u4e2d\uff1a\n    - initial_best = initial_gbest_f\uff08PSO\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u540e\u79cd\u7fa4\u7684\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    - best = gbest_f\uff08PSO\u8fd0\u884c\u7ed3\u675f\u540e\u7684\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    - opt = opt_val\uff08\u7406\u8bba\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    \n    \u8fd4\u56de\u622a\u65ad\u5230 [0, 1] \u7684 score\u3002\n    \"\"\"    \n    if abs(initial_gbest_f - opt_val) < 1e-12:\n        return 1.0 if gbest_f <= opt_val + 1e-12 else 0.0\n    \n    score = (initial_gbest_f - gbest_f) / (initial_gbest_f - opt_val)\n    return max(0.0, min(1.0, score))\n\n\ndef run_de_and_score(inputs, log_fn=print, mode='test', controller=None, seed=None):\n    \"\"\"\u8fd0\u884c DQN \u63a7\u5236\u7684 DE \u5e76\u8ba1\u7b97 RI \u8bc4\u5206\u3002\n    \n    Args:\n        inputs: dict from domains.bbob_unconstrained.utils.format_input_dict\n        Expected keys: 'problem_id','dimension','instance'\n        log_fn: logging function\n        mode: 'train' for training DQN (updates model), 'test' for evaluation only\n        controller: optional external controller (shared across problems)\n        seed: random seed for DE initialization\n    \n    Returns:\n        prediction (string) in format \"score|gbest_f|initial_gbest_f|opt_val\"\n    \"\"\"\n    # lazy import cocoex and DE module from metabbo\n    import cocoex\n\n    this_dir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), '..', '..', 'metabbo')\n    # \u5c06 metabbo \u76ee\u5f55\u6dfb\u52a0\u5230 sys.path\uff0c\u4ee5\u4fbf\u6a21\u5757\u5185\u7684\u76f8\u5bf9\u5bfc\u5165\u53ef\u4ee5\u6b63\u5e38\u5de5\u4f5c\n    if this_dir not in __import__('sys').path:\n        __import__('sys').path.insert(0, this_dir)\n    # load local ec_algorithm module\n    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('ec_mod', os.path.join(this_dir, 'ec_algorithm.py'))\n    ec_mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)\n    spec.loader.exec_module(ec_mod)\n    \n    # Create or use provided controller\n    if controller is None:\n        # load meta learning implementations\n        spec2 = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('meta_mod', os.path.join(this_dir, 'meta_learning.py'))\n        meta_mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec2)\n        spec2.loader.exec_module(meta_mod)\n        model_path = os.path.join(this_dir, 'dqn_model.pt')\n        controller = meta_mod.DiscreteDQNController(model_path=model_path, training=(mode == 'train'), seed=seed)\n    \n    # prepare suite filter (dimension + instance)\n    dim = int(inputs.get('dimension', 10))\n    inst = int(inputs.get('instance', 1))\n    max_evals = max(1000, dim * 500)\n\n    # \u4ece problem_id \u5b57\u7b26\u4e32\u4e2d\u63d0\u53d6 function \u7f16\u53f7\uff08\u4f8b\u5982 f1_i1 -> 1\uff09\n    problem_id_str = inputs.get('problem_id', 'f1_i1')\n    problem_id = int(str(problem_id_str).replace('f', '').split('_')[0])\n\n    suite = cocoex.Suite('bbob', '', f'dimensions: {dim} instance_indices:{inst} function_indices: {problem_id}')\n    # pick the matching problem from the suite\n    problem = next(iter(suite))\n\n    # run DE algorithm using DEOptimizer to get stats\n    try:\n        de_opt = ec_mod.DEOptimizer(problem.dimension, getattr(problem, 'lower_bounds', None), getattr(problem, 'upper_bounds', None), max_evals, params={'F': 0.5, 'CR': 0.5}, controller=controller, recorder=None, swarm_size=40)\n        # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4f1a\u66f4\u65b0 DQN \u6a21\u578b\uff0c\u6d4b\u8bd5\u6a21\u5f0f\u53ea\u4f7f\u7528\u6a21\u578b\n        gbest, stats = de_opt.optimize(problem, train=(mode == 'train'), seed=seed)\n        gbest_f = stats.get('gbest_f', None)\n        initial_gbest_f = stats.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f)\n        \n        # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4e0b\u4fdd\u5b58\u6a21\u578b\uff08\u53ea\u5728 controller \u662f\u5185\u90e8\u521b\u5efa\u65f6\u4fdd\u5b58\uff09\n        if mode == 'train' and controller is not None and hasattr(controller, 'net'):\n            import torch\n            model_path = os.path.join(this_dir, 'dqn_model.pt')\n            torch.save(controller.net.state_dict(), model_path)\n            log_fn(f\"[TRAIN] Model saved to {model_path}\")\n    except Exception as e:\n        return f'error: DE run failed: {e}'\n\n    # \u8ba1\u7b97 RI \u5206\u6570\n    # \u52a0\u8f7d\u7406\u8bba\u6700\u4f18\u503c\n    opt_vals = load_opt_vals()\n    opt_val_key = (problem_id, inst, dim)\n    opt_val = opt_vals.get(opt_val_key, None)\n    \n    # \u4f7f\u7528 RI \u8bc4\u5206\u65b9\u5f0f\n    score = compute_ri_score(initial_gbest_f, gbest_f, opt_val)\n    \n    mode_str = \"TRAIN\" if mode == 'train' else \"TEST\"\n    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} Score: {score}\")\n    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} gbest_f: {gbest_f}\")\n    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} initial_gbest_f: {initial_gbest_f}\")\n    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} opt_val: {opt_val}\")\n    \n    # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4e0b\u6536\u96c6 loss \u548c return\n    if mode == 'train':\n        training_loss = controller.get_total_loss() if hasattr(controller, 'get_total_loss') else None\n        sweep_return = controller.get_total_reward() if hasattr(controller, 'get_total_reward') else None\n        if training_loss is not None:\n            log_fn(f\"[TRAIN] Total DQN loss: {training_loss:.6f}\")\n        if sweep_return is not None:\n            log_fn(f\"[TRAIN] Total DQN return: {sweep_return:.4f}\")\n        return {\n            'score': score,\n            'gbest_f': gbest_f,\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n            'opt_val': opt_val,\n            'training_loss': training_loss,\n            'sweep_return': sweep_return,\n            'controller': controller\n        }\n    \n    # \u8fd4\u56de\u683c\u5f0f\uff1a\"score|gbest_f|initial_gbest_f|opt_val\" \u4ee5\u4fbf report.py \u89e3\u6790\n    prediction = f\"{score}|{gbest_f}|{initial_gbest_f}|{opt_val}\"\n\n    # \u4e5f\u8fd4\u56de dict \u683c\u5f0f\u4f9b\u6d4b\u8bd5\u811a\u672c\u4f7f\u7528\n    return {\n transition.\n   137\t        log_eps_start = float(np.log(self.epsilon_start))\n   138\t        log_eps_end = float(np.log(self.epsilon_end))\n   139\t        log_eps = log_eps_start + t * def __init__(self, w_start=0.9, w_end=0.4, decay_iters=100):\n    51\t        self.w_start = w_start\n    52\t        self.w_end = w_end\n    53\t        self.decay_iters = decay_iters\n    54\t\n Increase exploitation pressure (smaller pbest set) as the run progresses\n    69\t        pbest_p = max(0.05, 0.2 * (1.0 - t))\n    70\t        return {'w': w, 'c1': c1, 'c2': c2, 'F': F, 'CR': CR, 'pbest_p': pbest_p}\n    71\t\n    72\t\n    73\tclass AdaptiveDEController(ParamController):\n    74\t    \"\"\"Adaptive controller for DE/JADE algorithms with epsilon-constrained support.\n    75\t\n    76\t    - Early iterations: high F (exploration), high CR (diversity),\n    77\t      use 'rand/1' strategy to explore broadly.\n    78\t    - Late iterations: lower F (exploitation), moderate CR (retain\n    79\t      good building blocks), switch to 'jade' for fast convergence.\n    80\t    - Uses stagnation detection to temporarily boost F/CR.\n    81\t    - Provides a decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained handling,\n    82\t      allowing the optimizer to explore mildly infeasible regions early and\n    83\t      enforcing feasibility as the budget is \"\"\"\n    87\t    def __init__(self, f_start=0.9, f_end=0.3, cr_start=0.9, cr_end=0.4,\n    88\t                 strategy_change_iter=0.3, stagnation_boost=0.2,\n    89\t                 epsilon_start=1e-2, epsilon_end=1e-8):\n    90\t        self.f_start = f_start\n    91\t        self.f_end = f_end\n    92\t        self.cr_start = cr_start\n    93\t        self.cr_end = cr_end\n    94\t        self.strategy_change_iter = strategy_change_iter\n    95\t        self.stagnation_boost = stagnation_boost\n    96\t        self.epsilon_start = epsilon_start\n    97\t        self.epsilon_end = epsilon_end\n    98\t\n    99\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n   100\t        self.it = 0\n   101\t        self.last_gbest = None\n   102\t        self.last_gbest_cv = None\n   103\t        self.stagnation_count = 0\n   104\t\n   105\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n   106\t        self.it = 110\t        # Smooth progress-based decay from exploration to exploitation\n   111\t        F = self.f_start * (1 - t) + self.f_end * t\n   112\t        CR = self.cr_start * (1 - t) + self.cr_end * t\n   113\t\n   114\t        # Detect stagnation: no improvement in best fitness for several iterations\n   115\t        current_gbest = observation.get('gbest_f', None)\n   116\t        if else:\n   120\t                self.stagnation_count += 1\n   121\t        self.last_gbest = current_gbest\n   122\t\n   123\t        # If stagnated for a while, add extra diversity\n   124\t transition.\n   137\t        log_eps_start = float(np.log(self.epsilon_start))\n   138\t        log_eps_end = float(np.log(self.epsilon_end))\n   139\t        log_eps = log_eps_start + t * (log_eps_end - log_eps_start)\n   140\t        epsilon = float(np.exp(log_eps))\n   141\t\n   142\t        # If the current best is still infeasible late in the run, relax epsilon\n   143\t        # More exploitation as the budget is consumed\n            pbest_p = max(0.05, 0.2 * (1.0 - progress))\n            # Allow the controller to override the pbest selection pressure\n            if 'pbest_p' in params:\n                pbest_p = float(np.clip(float(params['pbest_p']), 0.01, 1.0))\n\n            # L-SHADE style linear population size reduction\n            if iteration > 1 and pop_size > 4:\n                target_size = max(4, int(self.pop_size * (1.0 - 0.6 * progress)))\n                if target_size < pop_size:\n                    keep_idx = np.argsort(fitness)[:target_size]\n                    pop = pop[keep_idx].copy()\n                    fitness = fitness[keep_idx].copy()\n                    pop_size = int(target_size)\n                    archive_capacity = max(pop_size, 20)\n\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n            pop_old = pop.copy()\n            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\n            S_F = []\n            S_CR = []\n            F_i_values = np.zeros(pop_size)\n            CR_i_values = np.zeros(pop_size)\n            \n            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n            pbest_num = max(1, int(pop_size * pbest_p))\n            pbest_indices = sorted_idx[:pbest_num]\n            \n            # Generate trial vectors using SHADE current-to-pbest/1\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                # Sample F_i from Cauchy distribution based on history memory\n                mi = int(rng.randint(memory_size))\n                mu_F = float(memory_F[mi]) * 0.5 + ctrl_F * 0.5\n                mu_CR = float(memory_CR[mi]) * 0.5 + ctrl_CR * 0.5\n                F_i = float(mu_F + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy())\n                if F_i <= 0.0:\n                    F_i = 0.1\n                elif F_i > 1.0:\n                    F_i = 1.0\n                CR_i = float(rng.normal(mu_CR, 0.1))\n                CR_i = max(0.0, min(1.0, CR_i))\n                F_i_values[i] = F_i\n                CR_i_values[i] = CR_i\n                \n                # Choose pbest from top pbest_p individuals\n                pbest_i = int(pbest_indices[int(rng.randint(len(pbest_indices)))])\n                pbest_x = pop[pbest_i]\n                \n                # current-to-pbest/1 mutation\n                base = pop[i] + F_i * (pbest_x - pop[i])\n                \n                # r1 from population, r2 from population or archive\n                r1 = int(rng.randint(pop_size))\n                while r1 == i:\n                    r1 = int(rng.randint(pop_size))\n                candidate_r2 = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i and idx != r1]\n                if len(archive) > 0:\n                    candidate_r2 += [pop_size + j for j in range(len(archive))]\n                if len(candidate_r2) < 1:\n                    r2 = int(rng.randint(pop_size))\n                    x2 = pop[r2]\n                else:\n                    r2 = int(candidate_r2[int(rng.randint(len(candidate_r2)))])\n                    if r2 >= pop_size:\n                        x2 = archive[r2 - pop_size]\n                    else:\n                        x2 = pop[r2]\n                \n                mutant = base + F_i * (pop[r1] - x2)\n                \n                # Binomial crossover\n                j_rand = int(rng.randint(self.dim))\n                mask = rng.random(self.dim) < CR_i\n                mask[j_rand] = True\n                trial = np.where(mask, mutant, pop[i])\n                trial = self._reflect(trial, lb, ub)\n                new_pop[i] = trial\n            \n            # Evaluate trial vectors and apply greedy selection\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                    done = True\n                    break\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n                evals += 1\n                \n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n                    # Add previous position to archive before replacement\n                    old_x = pop_old[i].reshape(1, -1)\n                    if len(archive) < archive_capacity:\n                        archive = np.vstack([archive, old_x])\n                    else:\n                        replace_idx = int(rng.randint(archive_capacity))\n                        archive[replace_idx] = old_x\n                    \n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n                    S_F.append(F_i_values[i])\n                    S_CR.append(CR_i_values[i])\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n            \n            # Update SHADE memory with successful parameters (weighted Lehmer mean for F)\n            if len(S_F) > 0:\n                S_F_arr = np.asarray(S_F, dtype=float)\n                S_CR_arr = np.asarray(S_CR, dtype=float)\n                sum_F = float(np.sum(S_F_arr))\n                if sum_F > 0:\n                    memory_F[memory_idx] = float(np.sum(S_F_arr * S_F_arr) / sum_F)\n                else:\n                    memory_F[memory_idx] = float(np.mean(S_F_arr))\n                memory_CR[memory_idx] = float(np.mean(S_CR_arr))\n                memory_idx = (memory_idx + 1) % memory_size\n\n            # Local search around the current best solution to accelerate convergence\n            if (iteration >= local_search_interval and iteration % local_search_interval == 0\n                    and evals < self.max_evals):\n                # Perturbation scale decreases as the run progresses\n                sigma_local = (ub - lb) * max(1e-7, 0.01 * (1.0 - 0.5 * progress))\n                for _ in range(local_search_n):\n                    if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                        break\n                    cand = self._reflect(gbest + sigma_local * rng.randn(self.dim), lb, ub)\n                    cand_f = evaluate_problem(problem, cand)\n                    evals += 1\n                    if cand_f < gbest_f:\n                        gbest_f = cand_f\n                        gbest = cand.copy()\n                        # Replace the worst individual with this improved point\n                        worst_i = int(np.argmax(fitness))\n                        pop[worst_i] = cand.copy()\n                        fitness[worst_i] = cand_f\n\n            # Stagnation detection: restart worst individuals if no improvement for several iterations\n            if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f - 1e-12:\n                stagnation_count = 0\n            else:\n                stagnation_count += 1\n            \n            if stagnation_count >= 5:\n                n_restart = max(1, int(pop_size * 0.15))\n                worst_idx = np.argsort(fitness)[-n_restart:]\n                sigma = (ub - lb) * 0.02 * (1.0 + progress)\n                for idx in worst_idx:\n                    if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                        break\n                    if rng.random() < 0.5:\n                        pop[idx] = self._reflect(gbest + sigma * rng.randn(self.dim), lb, ub)\n                    else:\n                        pop[idx] = rng.uniform(lb, ub, self.dim)\n                    fitness[idx] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[idx])\n                    evals += 1\n                    if fitness[idx] < gbest_f:\n                        gbest_f = fitness[idx]\n                        gbest = pop[idx].copy()\n                stagnation_count = 0\n            \n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n            \n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n            \n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n\n        self.stats = {\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\"\n    }\n    </json>",

If `path` is a directory, `view` lists non-hidden files and directories up to 2 levels deep\\n* The `create` command cannot be used if the specified `path` already exists as a file\\n* If a `command` generates a long output, it will be truncated and marked with `<response clipped>`\\n* The `undo_edit` command will revert the last edit made to the file at `path`\\n\\nNotes for using the `str_replace` command:\\n* The `old_str` parameter should match EXACTLY one or more consecutive lines from the original file. Setting `[start_line, -1]` shows all lines from `start_line` to the end of the file.', 'items': {'type': 'integer'}, 'type': 'array'}}, 'required': ['command', 'path']}}\n```\n\nYou can use tools in this format:\n<json>\n{\n    \"tool_name\": ...,\n    \"tool_input\": ...\n}\n</json>\n\nYou can use MULTIPLE tools in a single response by including multiple JSON blocks.\nSTRICTLY FOLLOW THE FORMAT OF TOOL_NAME AND TOOL_INPUT ABOVE.\nDO NOT HALLUCINATE OR MAKE UP ANYTHING.\n\n<json>\n    {\n        \"tool_name\": \"bash\",\n        \"tool_input\": {'command': \"sed -n '150,345p' /hada/metabbo/ec_algorithm.py\"},\n        \"tool_output\": \"archive_capacity = max(pop_size, 20)\n        stagnation_count = 0\n        local_search_interval = 10\n        local_search_n = max(4, min(dim, 8))\n        # Adaptive coordinate perturbation step sizes for local pattern search\n        pattern_step = (ub - lb) * 0.05\n        pattern_step = np.maximum(pattern_step, 1e-6 * (ub - lb))\n        \n        while not done:\n            iteration += 1\n            params = dict(self.params)\n            if self.controller is not None:\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n                    params.update(step_params)\n            \n            # Optional controller override (still supported, but SHADE also adapts internally)\n            ctrl_F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n            ctrl_CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n            \n            progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, self.max_evals))\n            # More exploitation as the budget is consumed\n            pbest_p = max(0.05, 0.2 * (1.0 - progress))\n            # Allow the controller to override the pbest selection pressure\n            if 'pbest_p' in params:\n                pbest_p = float(np.clip(float(params['pbest_p']), 0.01, 1.0))\n\n            # L-SHADE style linear population size reduction\n            if iteration > 1 and pop_size > 4:\n                target_size = max(4, int(self.pop_size * (1.0 - 0.6 * progress)))\n                if target_size < pop_size:\n                    keep_idx = np.argsort(fitness)[:target_size]\n                    pop = pop[keep_idx].copy()\n                    fitness = fitness[keep_idx].copy()\n                    pop_size = int(target_size)\n                    archive_capacity = max(pop_size, 20)\n\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n            pop_old = pop.copy()\n            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\n            S_F = []\n            S_CR = []\n            F_i_values = np.zeros(pop_size)\n            CR_i_values = np.zeros(pop_size)\n            \n            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n            pbest_num = max(1, int(pop_size * pbest_p))\n            pbest_indices = sorted_idx[:pbest_num]\n            \n            # Generate trial vectors using SHADE current-to-pbest/1\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                # Sample F_i from Cauchy distribution based on history memory\n                mi = int(rng.randint(memory_size))\n                mu_F = float(memory_F[mi]) * 0.5 + ctrl_F * 0.5\n                mu_CR = float(memory_CR[mi]) * 0.5 + ctrl_CR * 0.5\n                F_i = float(mu_F + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy())\n                if F_i <= 0.0:\n                    F_i = 0.1\n                elif F_i > 1.0:\n                    F_i = 1.0\n                CR_i = float(rng.normal(mu_CR, 0.1))\n                CR_i = max(0.0, min(1.0, CR_i))\n                F_i_values[i] = F_i\n                CR_i_values[i] = CR_i\n                \n                # Choose pbest from top pbest_p individuals\n                pbest_i = int(pbest_indices[int(rng.randint(len(pbest_indices)))])\n                pbest_x = pop[pbest_i]\n                \n                # current-to-pbest/1 mutation\n                base = pop[i] + F_i * (pbest_x - pop[i])\n                \n                # r1 from population, r2 from population or archive\n                r1 = int(rng.randint(pop_size))\n                while r1 == i:\n                    r1 = int(rng.randint(pop_size))\n                candidate_r2 = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i and idx != r1]\n                if len(archive) > 0:\n                    candidate_r2 += [pop_size + j for j in range(len(archive))]\n                if len(candidate_r2) < 1:\n                    r2 = int(rng.randint(pop_size))\n                    x2 = pop[r2]\n                else:\n                    r2 = int(candidate_r2[int(rng.randint(len(candidate_r2)))])\n                    if r2 >= pop_size:\n                        x2 = archive[r2 - pop_size]\n                    else:\n                        x2 = pop[r2]\n                \n                mutant = base + F_i * (pop[r1] - x2)\n                \n                # Binomial crossover\n                j_rand = int(rng.randint(self.dim))\n                mask = rng.random(self.dim) < CR_i\n                mask[j_rand] = True\n                trial = np.where(mask, mutant, pop[i])\n                trial = self._reflect(trial, lb, ub)\n                new_pop[i] = trial\n            \n            # Evaluate trial vectors and apply greedy selection\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                    done = True\n                    break\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n                evals += 1\n                \n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n                    # Add previous position to archive before replacement\n                    old_x = pop_old[i].reshape(1, -1)\n                    if len(archive) < archive_capacity:\n                        archive = np.vstack([archive, old_x])\n                    else:\n                        replace_idx = int(rng.randint(archive_capacity))\n                        archive[replace_idx] = old_x\n                    \n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n                    S_F.append(F_i_values[i])\n                    S_CR.append(CR_i_values[i])\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n            \n            # Update SHADE memory with successful parameters (weighted Lehmer mean for F)\n            if len(S_F) > 0:\n                S_F_arr = np.asarray(S_F, dtype=float)\n                S_CR_arr = np.asarray(S_CR, dtype=float)\n                sum_F = float(np.sum(S_F_arr))\n                if sum_F > 0:\n                    memory_F[memory_idx] = float(np.sum(S_F_arr * S_F_arr) / sum_F)\n                else:\n                    memory_F[memory_idx] = float(np.mean(S_F_arr))\n                memory_CR[memory_idx] = float(np.mean(S_CR_arr))\n                memory_idx = (memory_idx + 1) % memory_size\n\n            # Local search around the current best solution to accelerate convergence\n            if (iteration >= local_search_interval and iteration % local_search_interval == 0\n                    and evals < self.max_evals):\n                # Perturbation scale decreases as the run progresses\n                sigma_local = (ub - lb) * max(1e-7, 0.01 * (1.0 - 0.5 * progress))\n                for _ in range(local_search_n):\n                    if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                        break\n                    cand = self._reflect(gbest + sigma_local * rng.randn(self.dim), lb, ub)\n                    cand_f = evaluate_problem(problem, cand)\n                    evals += 1\n                    if cand_f < gbest_f:\n                        gbest_f = cand_f\n                        gbest = cand.copy()\n                        # Replace the worst individual with this improved point\n                        worst_i = int(np.argmax(fitness))\n                        pop[worst_i] = cand.copy()\n                        fitness[worst_i] = cand_f\n\n            # Stagnation detection: restart worst individuals if no improvement for several iterations\n            if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f - 1e-12:\n                stagnation_count = 0\n            else:\n                stagnation_count += 1\n            \n            if stagnation_count >= 5:\n                n_restart = max(1, int(pop_size * 0.15))\n                worst_idx = np.argsort(fitness)[-n_restart:]\n                sigma = (ub - lb) * 0.02 * (1.0 + progress)\n                for idx in worst_idx:\n                    if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                        break\n                    if rng.random() < 0.5:\n                        pop[idx] = self._reflect(gbest + sigma * rng.randn(self.dim), lb, ub)\n                    else:\n                        pop[idx] = rng.uniform(lb, ub, self.dim)\n                    fitness[idx] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[idx])\n                    evals += 1\n                    if fitness[idx] < gbest_f:\n                        gbest_f = fitness[idx]\n                        gbest = pop[idx].copy()\n                stagnation_count = 0\n            \n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n            \n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n            \n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n\n        self.stats = {\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\"\n    }\n    </json>"

Setting `[start_line, -1]` shows all lines from `start_line` to the end of the file.', 'items': {'type': 'integer'}, 'type': 'array'}}, 'required': ['command', 'path']}}\n```\n\nYou can use tools in /hada/domains/bbob_unconstrained/dataset.csv && echo '---' && sed -n '1,140p' /hada/domains/bbob_unconstrained/dqn_de_util.py\"},\n        \"tool_output\": \"problem_id,function,instance,dimension,opt_val,split\r\nf1_i1,1,1,20,79.48,train\r\nf2_i1,2,1,20,-209.88,train\r\nf5_i1,5,1,20,-9.21,train\r\nf8_i1,8,1,20,149.15,train\r\nf10_i1,10,1,20,-54.94,train\r\nf11_i1,11,1,20,76.27,train\r\nf12_i1,12,1,20,-621.11,train\r\nf15_i1,15,1,20,1000.0,train\r\nf3_i1,3,1,20,-462.09,test\r\nf4_i1,4,1,20,-462.09,test\r\nf6_i1,6,1,20,35.9,test\r\nf7_i1,7,1,20,92.94,test\r\nf9_i1,9,1,20,123.83,test\r\nf13_i1,13,1,20,29.97,test\r\nf14_i1,14,1,20,-52.35,test\r\nf16_i1,16,1,20,71.35,test\r\nf17_i1,17,1,20,-16.94,test\r\nf18_i1,18,1,20,-16.94,test\r\nf19_i1,19,1,20,-102.55,test\r\nf20_i1,20,1,20,-546.5,test\r\nf21_i1,21,1,20,40.78,test\r\nf22_i1,22,1,20,-1000.0,test\r\nf23_i1,23,1,20,6.87,test\r\nf24_i1,24,1,20,102.61,test---\nimport os\nimport importlib.util\nimport csv\nimport math\nimport numpy as np\n\n# Import global configuration\nimport sys\nsys.path.insert(0, '/hada')\nfrom config import set_global_seed, SEED, MODEL_NAME\n\n# Set global seed at module load time\nset_global_seed()\n\n\ndef load_opt_vals():\n    \"\"\"\u52a0\u8f7d dataset.csv \u6587\u4ef6\uff0c\u8fd4\u56de\u4e00\u4e2a\u5b57\u5178 {(function, instance, dimension): opt_val}\"\"\"\n    dataset_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), 'dataset.csv')\n    opt_vals = {}\n    if os.path.exists(dataset_path):\n        with open(dataset_path, 'r') as f:\n            reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n            for row in reader:\n                key = (int(row['function']), int(row['instance']), int(row['dimension']))\n                opt_vals[key] = float(row['opt_val'])\n    return opt_vals\n\n\ndef compute_ri_score(initial_gbest_f, gbest_f, opt_val):\n    \"\"\"\u8ba1\u7b97 score = (initial_best - best) / (initial_best - opt)\u3002\n    \n    \u5176\u4e2d\uff1a\n    - initial_best = initial_gbest_f\uff08PSO\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u540e\u79cd\u7fa4\u7684\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    - best = gbest_f\uff08PSO\u8fd0\u884c\u7ed3\u675f\u540e\u7684\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    - opt = opt_val\uff08\u7406\u8bba\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    \n    \u8fd4\u56de\u622a\u65ad\u5230 [0, 1] \u7684 score\u3002\n    \"\"\"    \n    if abs(initial_gbest_f - opt_val) < 1e-12:\n        return 1.0 if gbest_f <= opt_val + 1e-12 else 0.0\n    \n    score = (initial_gbest_f - gbest_f) / (initial_gbest_f - opt_val)\n    return max(0.0, min(1.0, score))\n\n\ndef run_de_and_score(inputs, log_fn=print, mode='test', controller=None, seed=None):\n    \"\"\"\u8fd0\u884c DQN \u63a7\u5236\u7684 DE \u5e76\u8ba1\u7b97 RI \u8bc4\u5206\u3002\n    \n    Args:\n        inputs: dict from domains.bbob_unconstrained.utils.format_input_dict\n        Expected keys: 'problem_id','dimension','instance'\n        log_fn: logging function\n        mode: 'train' for training DQN (updates model), 'test' for evaluation only\n        controller: optional external controller (shared across problems)\n        seed: random seed for DE initialization\n    \n    Returns:\n        prediction (string) in format \"score|gbest_f|initial_gbest_f|opt_val\"\n    \"\"\"\n    # lazy import cocoex and DE module from metabbo\n    import cocoex\n\n    this_dir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), '..', '..', 'metabbo')\n    # \u5c06 metabbo \u76ee\u5f55\u6dfb\u52a0\u5230 sys.path\uff0c\u4ee5\u4fbf\u6a21\u5757\u5185\u7684\u76f8\u5bf9\u5bfc\u5165\u53ef\u4ee5\u6b63\u5e38\u5de5\u4f5c\n    if this_dir not in __import__('sys').path:\n        __import__('sys').path.insert(0, this_dir)\n    # load local ec_algorithm module\n    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('ec_mod', os.path.join(this_dir, 'ec_algorithm.py'))\n    ec_mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)\n    spec.loader.exec_module(ec_mod)\n    \n    # Create or use provided controller\n    if controller is None:\n        # load meta learning implementations\n        spec2 = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('meta_mod', os.path.join(this_dir, 'meta_learning.py'))\n        meta_mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec2)\n        spec2.loader.exec_module(meta_mod)\n        model_path = os.path.join(this_dir, 'dqn_model.pt')\n        controller = meta_mod.DiscreteDQNController(model_path=model_path, training=(mode == 'train'), seed=seed)\n    \n    # prepare suite filter (dimension + instance)\n    dim = int(inputs.get('dimension', 10))\n    inst = int(inputs.get('instance', 1))\n    max_evals = max(1000, dim * 500)\n\n    # \u4ece problem_id \u5b57\u7b26\u4e32\u4e2d\u63d0\u53d6 function \u7f16\u53f7\uff08\u4f8b\u5982 f1_i1 -> 1\uff09\n    problem_id_str = inputs.get('problem_id', 'f1_i1')\n    problem_id = int(str(problem_id_str).replace('f', '').split('_')[0])\n\n    suite = cocoex.Suite('bbob', '', f'dimensions: {dim} instance_indices:{inst} function_indices: {problem_id}')\n    # pick the matching problem from the suite\n    problem = next(iter(suite))\n\n    # run DE algorithm using DEOptimizer to get stats\n    try:\n        de_opt = ec_mod.DEOptimizer(problem.dimension, getattr(problem, 'lower_bounds', None), getattr(problem, 'upper_bounds', None), max_evals, params={'F': 0.5, 'CR': 0.5}, controller=controller, recorder=None, swarm_size=40)\n        # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4f1a\u66f4\u65b0 DQN \u6a21\u578b\uff0c\u6d4b\u8bd5\u6a21\u5f0f\u53ea\u4f7f\u7528\u6a21\u578b\n        gbest, stats = de_opt.optimize(problem, train=(mode == 'train'), seed=seed)\n        gbest_f = stats.get('gbest_f', None)\n        initial_gbest_f = stats.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f)\n        \n        # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4e0b\u4fdd\u5b58\u6a21\u578b\uff08\u53ea\u5728 controller \u662f\u5185\u90e8\u521b\u5efa\u65f6\u4fdd\u5b58\uff09\n        if mode == 'train' and controller is not None and hasattr(controller, 'net'):\n            import torch\n            model_path = os.path.join(this_dir, 'dqn_model.pt')\n            torch.save(controller.net.state_dict(), model_path)\n            log_fn(f\"[TRAIN] Model saved to {model_path}\")\n    except Exception as e:\n        return f'error: DE run failed: {e}'\n\n    # \u8ba1\u7b97 RI \u5206\u6570\n    # \u52a0\u8f7d\u7406\u8bba\u6700\u4f18\u503c\n    opt_vals = load_opt_vals()\n    opt_val_key = (problem_id, inst, dim)\n    opt_val = opt_vals.get(opt_val_key, None)\n    \n    # \u4f7f\u7528 RI \u8bc4\u5206\u65b9\u5f0f\n    score = compute_ri_score(initial_gbest_f, gbest_f, opt_val)\n    \n    mode_str = \"TRAIN\" if mode == 'train' else \"TEST\"\n    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} Score: {score}\")\n    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} gbest_f: {gbest_f}\")\n    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} initial_gbest_f: {initial_gbest_f}\")\n    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} opt_val: {opt_val}\")\n    \n    # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4e0b\u6536\u96c6 loss \u548c return\n    if mode == 'train':\n        training_loss = controller.get_total_loss() if hasattr(controller, 'get_total_loss') else None\n        sweep_return = controller.get_total_reward() if hasattr(controller, 'get_total_reward') else None\n        if training_loss is not None:\n            log_fn(f\"[TRAIN] Total DQN loss: {training_loss:.6f}\")\n        if sweep_return is not None:\n            log_fn(f\"[TRAIN] Total DQN return: {sweep_return:.4f}\")\n        return {\n            'score': score,\n            'gbest_f': gbest_f,\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n            'opt_val': opt_val,\n            'training_loss': training_loss,\n            'sweep_return': sweep_return,\"\n    }\n    </json>",

         opt_vals[key] = float(row['opt_val'])\n    return opt_vals\n\n\ndef compute_ri_score(initial_gbest_f, gbest_f, opt_val):\n    \"\"\"\u8ba1\u7b97 score = (initial_best - best) / (initial_best - opt)\u3002\n    \n    \u5176\u4e2d\uff1a\n    - initial_best = initial_gbest_f\uff08PSO\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u540e\u79cd\u7fa4\u7684\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    - best = gbest_f\uff08PSO\u8fd0\u884c\u7ed3\u675f\u540e\u7684\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    - opt = opt_val\uff08\u7406\u8bba\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    \n    \u8fd4\u56de\u622a\u65ad\u5230 [0, 1] \u7684 score\u3002\n    \"\"\"    \n    if abs(initial_gbest_f - opt_val) < 1e-12:\n        return 1.0 if gbest_f <= opt_val + 1e-12 else 0.0\n    \n    score = (initial_gbest_f - gbest_f) / (initial_gbest_f - opt_val)\n    return max(0.0, min(1.0, score))\n\n\ndef run_de_and_score(inputs, log_fn=print, mode='test', controller=None, seed=None):\n    \"\"\"\u8fd0\u884c DQN \u63a7\u5236\u7684 DE     spec2 = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('meta_mod', os.path.join(this_dir, 'meta_learning.py'))\n        meta_mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec2)\n seed=seed)\n    \n    # prepare suite filter (dimension + instance)\n    dim = int(inputs.get('dimension', 10))\n    inst = int(inputs.get('instance', 1))\n    max_evals = max(1000, dim * 500)\n\n    # \u4ece problem_id \u5b57\u7b26\u4e32\u4e2d\u63d0\u53d6 function \u7f16\u53f7\uff08\u4f8b\u5982 f1_i1 -> 1\uff09\n    problem_id_str = inputs.get('problem_id', 'f1_i1')\n    problem_id = int(str(problem_id_str).replace('f', '').split('_')[0])\n\n    suite = cocoex.Suite('bbob', '', f'dimensions: {dim} instance_indices:{inst} function_indices: {problem_id}')\n    # pick the matching problem from the suite\n    problem = next(iter(suite))\n\n    # run DE algorithm using DEOptimizer to get stats\n    try:\n        de_opt = ec_mod.DEOptimizer(problem.dimension, getattr(problem, 'lower_bounds', None), getattr(problem, 'upper_bounds', None), max_evals, params={'F': 0.5, 'CR': 0.5}, controller=controller, recorder=None, swarm_size=40)\n        # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4f1a\u66f4\u65b0 DQN \u6a21\u578b\uff0c\u6d4b\u8bd5\u6a21\u5f0f\u53ea\u4f7f\u7528\u6a21\u578b\n        gbest, stats = de_opt.optimize(problem, train=(mode == 'train'), seed=seed)\n        gbest_f = stats.get('gbest_f', None)\n        initial_gbest_f = stats.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f)\n        \n        # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4e0b\u4fdd\u5b58\u6a21\u578b\uff08\u53ea\u5728 controller \u662f\u5185\u90e8\u521b\u5efa\u65f6\u4fdd\u5b58\uff09\n        if mode == 'train' and controller is not None and hasattr(controller, 'net'):\n            import torch\n            model_path = os.path.join(this_dir, 'dqn_model.pt')\n            torch.save(controller.net.state_dict(), model_path)\n            log_fn(f\"[TRAIN] Model saved to {model_path}\")\n    except Exception as e:\n        return f'error: DE run failed: {e}'\n\n    # \u8ba1\u7b97 RI \u5206\u6570\n    # f{problem_id} opt_val: {opt_val}\")\n    \n    # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4e0b\u6536\u96c6 loss \u548c return\n    if mode == 'train':\n        training_loss = controller.get_total_loss() if hasattr(controller, 'get_total_loss') else None\n        sweep_return = controller.get_total_reward() if hasattr(controller, 'get_total_reward') else None\n        if training_loss is not None:\n            log_fn(f\"[TRAIN] Total DQN loss: {training_loss:.6f}\")\n        if sweep_return is not None:\n            log_fn(f\"[TRAIN] Total DQN return: {sweep_return:.4f}\")\n        return {\n            'score': score,\n            'gbest_f': gbest_f,\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n            'opt_val': opt_val,\n            'training_loss': training_loss,\n            'sweep_return': sweep_return,\"\n    }\n    </json>"
